Executive Summary & Problem Context\n\n### The Problem\nThe repository root accumulated one-off Markdown artifacts from completed stages and tasks:\n- Stage/review write-ups (`IMPLEMENTATION_SUMMARY.md`, `PR_REVIEW_SUMMARY.md`, `REVIEW_SUMMARY.md`, `STAGE_REVIEW_SUMMARY.md`, `STAGE_VERDICT.md`, `VERDICT_WP5.md`, `KAINBAIN_FINAL_VERDICT.txt`)\n- Superseded planning documents (`phase2_completion_plan.md`, `phase2_plan.md`)\n- An archived research scratch file (`research/phase0-branch-dry-run.md`)\n- Accidentally committed runner session transcripts (`*.jsonl` at root)\n\nThese files are not live documentation: none are referenced by runtime code, user-facing docs, or CI gates. They clutter the root, confuse entry-point discovery, and obscure which Markdown files are authoritative.\n\nThe goal is a *general* cleanup: determine which root Markdown files are actually used, keep those in place, and remove (not relocate) the genuinely unused ones. Relocation to `/docs` is intentionally **not** the default because `/docs` is gitignored on this repository (local-only until WP-2 lands env-driven resolution); moving live-orphaned files there would hide them from fresh clones and from the lint/link gates.\n\n### Primary Objective\nProduce a clean, auditable repository root containing only Markdown files that are either:\n1. Canonical entry points / process documents (referenced and gated), or\n2. Deliberately retained product/deployment documentation (linked from `README.md`/`DEPRECATIONS.md`).\n\nAll other Markdown artifacts must be deleted via `git rm`, with no references left dangling.\n\n### Secondary Objectives\n- Keep the cleanup **scoped and reversible**: deletion must be driven by an explicit reference inventory, never by filename heuristics alone.\n- Preserve or improve the existing documentation quality gates (`scripts/lint-agents-md.sh`, `scripts/check-doc-links.mjs`).\n- Avoid introducing new link failures: the 21 pre-existing stale `docs/...` references must not increase.\n- Leave the build/runtime/test behavior unchanged (Markdown deletions are documentation-only).\n\n---\n\n## 2. Survey Analysis\n\nThe task metadata records a survey pending flag (`surveyFile: SURVEY.md`) that was completed with **no answers** (\"Survey completed (none)\"). Consequently, `SURVEY.md` does not exist in this task folder and there are no questionnaire selections to factor verbatim.\n\n| Survey Input | State | Architectural Realization in This Plan |\n| :--- | :--- | :--- |\n| `SURVEY.md` | Absent (survey result: none) | No interactive questionnaire was answered. Defaults applied: keep all *referenced* root Markdown, delete *unreferenced* artifacts, do not move anything into the gitignored `docs/` tree. |\n| User-provided free-text title | \"lots of unused md files in the root ... moved to docs or (more likely deleted)\" | Deletion is the default for unreferenced artifacts; relocation to `docs/` is rejected because `/docs` is gitignored and host-local. |\n| \"make change easy then make the change\" (user principle) | Required | Phase 0 builds a reference inventory and read-only verification harness *before* any deletion; deletions are batched and reversible. |\n\n### Known Constraints (from repo policy)\n- `/docs` is gitignored (`/docs` in `.gitignore`) and is host-local task storage; it must not become the canonical home of repo docs.\n- `site-docs/` is user-facing product documentation; stale stage verdicts/deployment notes do not belong there (and duplication would be misleading).\n- Root entry-point docs are gated: `scripts/lint-agents-md.sh` enforces 26 checks; `scripts/check-doc-links.mjs` validates links (with 21 known stale `docs/...` entries, pre-existing and unrelated).\n\n---\n\n## 3. Guiding Principles & Standards Alignment\n\n### \"Make Change Easy, Then Make the Change\" (Kent Beck)\n1. **First (Make Change Easy):**\n   - Build a complete, machine-readable inventory of root `*.md` files and every inbound reference to them (code, tests, scripts, workflows, other docs).\n   - Run the existing gates (lint-agents-md, check-doc-links) to establish a numbered, reproducible baseline before touching anything.\n   - Classify each root Markdown file as `KEEP-LIVE`, `KEEP-BY-POLICY`, or `DELETE-UNUSED` with explicit evidence.\n   - Add a small, read-only audit helper (optional) so future regressions are caught cheaply.\n2. **Then (Make the Change):**\n   - Delete `DELETE-UNUSED` files with `git rm` in a single, reviewable batch.\n   - Remove any now-empty directories.\n   - Fix accidental artifacts committed to root (session transcripts) and prevent recurrence via `.gitignore`.\n   - Keep all `KEEP-*` files untouched so no live links break.\n\n### Reliability & Risk Alignment\n- **Escalation Ladder Level 1 (Preventative Design):** Static detection at the earliest gate prevents \"cleanup by guesswork\" and avoids deleting something that a workflow references untracked.\n- **Fail-safe:** Markdown deletion cannot break runtime. The only regression surface is doc/link gates; both are run before and after.\n\n### KainBain Code Standards Alignment\n- **Readability over cleverness:** Use plain filenames and explicit evidence (reference counts) rather than heuristic pattern matching.\n- **Co-location of tests:** Verification uses existing scripts under `scripts/` and `tests/`; no new runtime code is required.\n- **Pure, deterministic audit outputs:** Any helper prints sorted, stable output with paths and reference counts.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Current-State Inventory (Baseline)\n\nInventory taken on the task branch at HEAD `75c4d45`. Root Markdown count after prior cleanup passes: **6 files**, all classified as live.\n\n| Root File | Classification | Evidence (inbound references) |\n| :--- | :--- | :--- |\n| `README.md` | `KEEP-LIVE` (entry point) | `agents.md`, `PORTABLE_DEPLOYMENT.md`, frontend tests, canary-e2e, `scripts/check-doc-links.mjs`, `site-docs/README.md` |\n| `agents.md` | `KEEP-LIVE` (agent instructions) | `README.md`, `AGENTS-MAP.md`, `scripts/lint-agents-md.sh`, `scripts/check-doc-links.mjs`, `.pi-lens.json`, `api/siteDocs.integrity.test.js` |\n| `AGENTS-MAP.md` | `KEEP-LIVE` | `agents.md`, `README.md`, `scripts/lint-agents-md.sh`, `scripts/check-doc-links.mjs`, `scripts/check-one-function-per-file.mjs`, `scripts/check-jsdoc-example.mjs`, `CHANGELOG.md` |\n| `CHANGELOG.md` | `KEEP-LIVE` (release notes) | `README.md`, `scripts/check-doc-links.mjs` |\n| `DEPRECATIONS.md` | `KEEP-LIVE` (dep-audit gate) | `README.md`, `frontend/eslint.config.js`, `frontend/scripts/check-no-deprecations.js`, `scripts/check-doc-links.mjs`, `scripts/check-dep-audit.mjs`, `scripts/dep-audit-allowlist.json`, `api/project/stageNormalizers.js` |\n| `PORTABLE_DEPLOYMENT.md` | `KEEP-LIVE` | `README.md` (twice), `DEPRECATIONS.md` |\n\nPreviously deleted (from prior requirement-refinement pass, commit `a1a44c2` / `357bc6e`), recorded for traceability:\n- `IMPLEMENTATION_SUMMARY.md`, `PR_REVIEW_SUMMARY.md`, `REVIEW_SUMMARY.md`, `STAGE_REVIEW_SUMMARY.md`, `STAGE_VERDICT.md`, `VERDICT_WP5.md`, `KAINBAIN_FINAL_VERDICT.txt`, `phase2_completion_plan.md`, `phase2_plan.md` — one-off feature/review/planning artifacts for finished work.\n- `research/phase0-branch-dry-run.md` (and its now-empty `research/` directory) — unreferenced scratch superseded by `scripts/verify-git-foundation.js`.\n- `2026-10-27T09-33-47-107Z_*.jsonl` — accidentally committed runner session transcript.\n\n### Gate Baseline (must not regress)\n- `bash scripts/lint-agents-md.sh` → **PASS (26 checks)**.\n- `node scripts/check-doc-links.mjs` → **21 stale `docs/...` references** (pre-existing; unrelated to root Markdown cleanup; must not increase).\n- Root `*.jsonl` artifacts: removed; pattern now guarded by `/2*.jsonl` in `.gitignore`.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Step-by-Step Implementation Workflow\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart TD\n    subgraph Phase 0: Research & Inventory\n        R1[Enumerate root *.md] --> R2[Build inbound reference map]\n        R2 --> R3[Run gates to capture baseline]\n        R3 --> R4[Classify each file: KEEP-LIVE / KEEP-BY-POLICY / DELETE-UNUSED]\n    end\n\n    subgraph Phase 1: Make Change Easy\n        M1[Document inventory + rationale in this plan] --> M2[Optional read-only audit helper scripts/check-root-md.mjs]\n        M2 --> M3[Confirm deletion list has zero inbound refs]\n    end\n\n    subgraph Phase 2: Make the Change\n        C1[git rm DELETE-UNUSED batch] --> C2[Remove now-empty directories]\n        C2 --> C3[Remove accidentally committed *.jsonl; add /2*.jsonl to .gitignore]\n    end\n\n    subgraph Phase 3: Link & Entry-Point Hygiene\n        L1[Verify lint-agents-md still PASS] --> L2[Verify check-doc-links count unchanged or improved]\n        L2 --> L3[Confirm README/AGENTS-MAP link tables updated only if needed]\n    end\n\n    subgraph Phase 4: Verification & Rollback Readiness\n        V1[git status and git diff --stat review] --> V2[Run npm run test:unit as guard]\n        V2 --> V3[Document rollback: git restore on any failure]\n    end\n\n    Phase 0 --> Phase 1 --> Phase 2 --> Phase 3 --> Phase 4\n```\n\n### Work Package 0 — Research & Inventory (early; read-only)\n**Objective:** Determine, with evidence, which root Markdown files are unused.\n\n1. Enumerate: `ls -1 *.md` (root only; scoped to avoid picking up md in subdirectories).\n2. For each candidate, collect inbound references across all tracked file types:\n   - Markdown: `grep -R --include='*.md' \"<filename>\"`\n   - Code/tests: `grep -R --include='*.js' --include='*.jsx' --include='*.mjs' --include='*.ts' --include='*.tsx' --include='*.json' --include='*.sh' \"<filename>\"`\n   - Workflows/CI: `.github/workflows/*`, `package.json` scripts, `scripts/run-tests.mjs`.\n3. Check git provenance: `git log --oneline -- <file>` (distinguish committed stage artifacts from original project docs).\n4. Check gates:\n   - `bash scripts/lint-agents-md.sh` (record pass/fail + count)\n   - `node scripts/check-doc-links.mjs` (record failure count)\n5. Classify:\n   - `KEEP-LIVE` if any inbound reference from code/tests/scripts/gates/docs.\n   - `KEEP-BY-POLICY` if unreferenced but canonical (e.g., `PORTABLE_DEPLOYMENT.md` is kept because linked from README even though move would be tempting).\n   - `DELETE-UNUSED` only if zero inbound references **and** no canonical role.\n\n**Deliverable:** A table (as in §4) committed in the plan/report, with reference counts.\n\n### Work Package 1 — Make Change Easy\n**Objective:** Make the deletion batch safe and repeatable; do not delete yet.\n\n1. Record the baseline (Work Package 0 table) in this plan and in the stage report.\n2. (Optional, recommended) Add `scripts/check-root-md.mjs` (read-only):\n   - Lists root `*.md` files.\n   - For each, reports inbound reference count and files.\n   - Exits 0 always (informational); exits 1 only on usage error.\n   - This is a *scanner*, not a gate, and must not delete anything.\n3. Re-verify the `DELETE-UNUSED` list has zero references after any concurrent changes.\n4. Confirm `.gitignore` patterns that protect root artifacts (`/2*.jsonl`) are present and root-anchored, verified against fixture `.jsonl` files under `api/agent/harnesses/` (must remain unignored).\n\n### Work Package 2 — Make the Change\n**Objective:** Execute the cleanup.\n\n1. Delete the `DELETE-UNUSED` batch with `git rm`:\n   ```bash\n   IMPLEMENTATION_SUMMARY.md\n   PR_REVIEW_SUMMARY.md\n   REVIEW_SUMMARY.md\n   STAGE_REVIEW_SUMMARY.md\n   STAGE_VERDICT.md\n   VERDICT_WP5.md\n   KAINBAIN_FINAL_VERDICT.txt\n   phase2_completion_plan.md\n   phase2_plan.md\n   research/phase0-branch-dry-run.md\n   ```\n2. Remove the accidental artifact (if present) and guard future recurrence:\n   - `git rm 2026-10-27T09-33-47-107Z_*.jsonl`\n   - Already-implemented `.gitignore` rule: `/2*.jsonl` (root-anchored; verified against tracked harness fixtures).\n3. Remove now-empty directories (e.g., `research/`) to avoid an empty folder.\n4. **Do not** move any file into `/docs` or `site-docs/`. If a file is arguably useful long-term, promote it deliberately into `site-docs/` or `agent-docs/` via a separate PR with explicit justification; ad-hoc relocation is out of scope.\n\n### Work Package 3 — Link & Entry-Point Hygiene (post-change verification)\n1. Run `bash scripts/lint-agents-md.sh` → expect PASS (26 checks). Any failure means a deleted file was referenced by `AGENTS-MAP.md`, `agents.md`, `README.md`, or a script — restore that file and re-classify.\n2. Run `node scripts/check-doc-links.mjs` → count must equal the baseline (21) or be lower; it must not increase. New entries attributable to this change are forbidden.\n3. If root entry-point tables in `AGENTS-MAP.md`/`README.md` enumerate Markdown files, reconcile them (preferably leaving them out of per-file lists, to avoid future churn).\n\n### Work Package 4 — Verification Plan\n**Automated:**\n1. `bash scripts/lint-agents-md.sh` (pass count unchanged).\n2. `node scripts/check-doc-links.mjs` (failure count ≤ baseline).\n3. `npm run test:unit` (or `npm test`) as a guard; Markdown deletions must not affect compilation/test discovery, but running the unit gate confirms no accidental collateral damage.\n4. `git status` shows only intended deletions + `.gitignore` change; `git diff --stat` reviewed.\n5. `git ls-files '*.md' | grep -E '^[^/]+\\.md$'` after cleanup equals exactly the 6 `KEEP-LIVE`/`KEEP-BY-POLICY` files (or a future, explicitly justified set).\n\n**Red-stage (failure-path) tests:**\n1. Temporarily revive one deleted artifact via `git show <sha>:<file> > /tmp/test.md` and confirm the inventory classifies it as `DELETE-UNUSED` again (no reference appears).\n2. Inject a synthetic root Markdown file with a unique name and verify the audit lists it as `DELETE-UNUSED` (if the helper from Work Package 1 is added).\n\n**Rollback:**\n- `git restore --source=HEAD~1 -- <path>` for any file deleted in error, or simply revert the cleanup commit. Because the deleted files are unreferenced Markdown, rollback is safe and does not affect runtime.\n\n### Work Package 5 — Documentation & Maintenance\n1. JSDoc/doc-comment standards apply only if new code (the optional helper) is added; otherwise no code change occurs.\n2. Record completion in the stage/task report, including:\n   - Before/after root Markdown list and count.\n   - Gate results.\n   - Commit SHA of the cleanup.\n3. Update `CHANGELOG.md` only if the project's release-notes convention treats root-level doc cleanup as user-facing; otherwise leave it to avoid noise (prior passes did not add entries for this cleanup).\n4. Created on first up.\\n      - ./projects:/workspace/projects\\n      # Container state: notes space, sessions, caches (named volume).\\n      - kainbain-data:/home/node/.kainbain\\n    env_file:\\n      # Optional: absent on fresh clones (required: false), secrets only.\\n      - path: .env\\n        required: false\\n    environment:\\n      NODE_ENV: production\\n      PORT: \"3100\"\\n      PROJECTS_ROOT: /workspace/projects\\n      NOTES_SPACE_ROOT: /home/node/.kainbain/notes\\n      KAINBAIN_DIST: /app/frontend/dist\\n      # Lockstep stamps (D7): same commit must reach both images.\\n      KAINBAIN_BUILD_REF: ${KAINBAIN_BUILD_REF:-dev}\\n      KAINBAIN_BUILD_NUMBER: ${KAINBAIN_BUILD_NUMBER:-0}\\n      # Optional: force the mock harness (no CLI needed) instead of the\\n      # default. Leave unset to follow KAINBAIN_DEFAULT_HARNESS (default pi).\\n      # KAINBAIN_DEFAULT_HARNESS: mock\\n    restart: unless-stopped\\n\\n  frontend:\\n    build:\\n      context: .\\n      dockerfile: frontend/Dockerfile\\n      args:\\n        # Empty = same origin (the UI calls the origin nginx serves it\\n        # from; nginx proxies API + /ws to the backend).\\n        VITE_API_BASE: \"\"\\n        VITE_WS_URL: \"\"\\n        VITE_BUILD_REF: ${KAINBAIN_BUILD_REF:-dev}\\n        VITE_NOTES_VIEWER_URL: ${VITE_NOTES_VIEWER_URL:-}\\n    image: kainbain-frontend:latest\\n    ports:\\n      - \"${KAINBAIN_PORT:-3100}:80\"\\n    depends_on:\\n      - backend\\n    restart: unless-stopped\\n\\nvolumes:\\n  kainbain-data:\\n',\n    expected: /\\$\\{KAINBAIN_PORT:-3100\\}:3100/,\n    operator: 'match',\n    diff: 'simple'\n  }\n----------------------------------------------------------------------\nFast gate complete — starting Canary Tests alone (serial phase, no shared CPUs with the unit suites)...\n▸ started Canary Tests\n⏳ still running: Canary Tests (30s)\n⏳ still running: Canary Tests (1m00s)\n⏳ still running: Canary Tests (1m30s)\n⏳ still running: Canary Tests (2m00s)\n⏳ still running: Canary Tests (2m30s)\n⏳ still running: Canary Tests (3m00s)\n⏳ still running: Canary Tests (3m30s)\n⏳ still running: Canary Tests (4m00s)\n⏳ still running: Canary Tests (4m30s)","meta":{"source":"process-log"}},{"id":"log-25714","ts":"2026-10-27T12:16:25.994Z","feed":"main","feeds":["main"],"kind":"system","turn":null,"text":"[INFO] Stage run aborted by operator in 'Automated tests' — awaiting resume/retry","meta":{"source":"process-log"}},{"id":"log-25710","ts":"2026-10-27T14:42:45.686Z","feed":"main","feeds":["main"],"kind":"system","turn":null,"text":"[INFO] Advanced from stage 'Review Plan' to stage 'catchup'","meta":{"source":"process-log"}},{"id":"log-25711","ts":"2026-10-27T14:42:45.796Z","feed":"main","feeds":["main"],"kind":"system","turn":null,"text":"[GIT] Task-branch ref 'kb/c76e13f5-4453-4d1c-85c2-d9d8f9fee0b5' already exists — no checkout performed (human checkpoint 'Review Plan';
